without limitation, controls administered pursuant to the U.S. Export
Administration Regulations (15 C.F.R. parts 730-774), EU and EU
Member State export controls administered pursuant to the EU Dual Use

supplement to 15 C.F.R. part 746 (e.g., as industrial goods or luxury

项，但不包括被归类为EAR99 且未列入15 C.F.R.第746 部分任何附
件（如工业品或奢侈品）的受管制物项，亦不包括其ECCN 仅因反恐
（AT）原因受控且未在15 C.F.R. §746.5(a)(1)中列明的受管制物项；
或(ii) 在本合同项下直接或间接提供给公司或为公司利益提供时，根
据适用的出口管制法律需取得许可证、批准或其他授权的受管制物
项。

TERMS AND CONDITIONS”) shall apply as part of the AGREEMENT in accordance with Clause 2 of the

本通用条款与条件，可能会不时进行修订（“通用条款与条件”）应根据合同协议书第2 条的规定，作

1.
DEFINITIONS AND INTERPRETATION 定义与解释
DEFINITIONS
定义
In these GENERAL TERMS AND CONDITIONS, unless otherwise defined, capitalised words and
expressions shall have the following meanings:
在本通用条款与条件中，除另有定义外，大写的词语和术语应具有以下含义：
“ABANDONMENT” means:
“弃工”指：
(a)
a deliberate abandonment of the WORKS (except where the WORKS are suspended under the
AGREEMENT or due to a FORCE MAJEURE EVENT); or
故意放弃合同工程（但在合同工程根据本合同或因不可抗力事件暂停时除外）；或
(b)
any material failure to perform the WORKS provided that: (i) the CONTRACTOR is unable
to provide to the COMPANY, within fifteen (15) DAYS of being requested to do so, a written
confirmation, together with supporting evidence, demonstrating that there is a reasonable
prospect of the CONTRACTOR achieving PROVISIONAL ACCEPTANCE within one
hundred and eighty (180) DAYS of the PROVISIONAL ACCEPTANCE DATE and (ii) the
COMPANY is not in breach of its obligations set out in Articles 7.1, 23, 24, 37, 41 and 42.
实质性未能履行合同工程，且符合以下条件：（i）承包商在收到公司要求后十五（15）
日内，未能向公司提供书面确认及支持性证据，以证明承包商有合理可能在初步验收日
期后一百八十（180）日内实现初步验收；并且（ii）公司并未违反其在第7.1 条、第23
条、第24 条、第37 条、第41 条及第42 条项下的义务。
“ABU DHABI” means the Emirate of Abu Dhabi (including its territorial waters).
“阿布扎比”指阿布扎比酋长国（包括其领海）。
“ADNOC” means Abu Dhabi National Oil Company (ADNOC) P.J.S.C..
“ADNOC”指阿布扎比国家石油公司。
“ADNOC GROUP” means ADNOC and each PERSON directly or indirectly CONTROLLED by
ADNOC.
“ADNOC 集团”指ADNOC 及所有由ADNOC 直接或间接控制的主体。
“ADNOC GROUP SUPPLIER & PARTNER CODE OF ETHICS” means the conduct policy for
suppliers and partners of the ADNOC GROUP, as amended from time to time, which is available at

“ANNEXURE” means one of the annexures set out in Clause 1.3(b) of the FORM OF AGREEMENT.
“附件”，指合同协议书第1.3(b)条所列附件。

“ANTI-BRIBERY LAWS” means any law, rule, regulation, or other legally binding measure of any
jurisdiction that relates to bribery or corruption.
“反贿赂法律”，指任何司法管辖区内与反贿赂或反腐败有关的法律、规则、法规或其他具有法
律约束力的措施。
“ANTI-MONEY LAUNDERING LAWS” means applicable laws, including common or civil law,
regulations, rules and guidance having the force of law criminalizing or imposing administrative or
regulatory obligations and/or liability in respect of money laundering.
‘Money laundering’ means the
acquisition, possession, use, conversion, transfer or concealment of the true nature of property of any
description, and legal documents or instruments evidencing title to, or interest in, such property,
knowing that such property is an economic advantage from criminal offences, for the purpose of:
“反洗钱法律”指适用的法律（包括普通法或大陆法）、法规、规则以及具有法律效力的指南，
其内容将洗钱行为入罪，或就洗钱事项设定行政管理或监管方面的义务及/或法律责任。“洗钱”
指在明知相关财产系来源于刑事犯罪的情况下，为以下目的而对任何性质的财产及证明该财产所
有权或权益的法律文件或文书进行获取、持有、使用、转换、转移或隐匿其真实性质的行为：
(a)
concealing or disguising the illicit origin of the property (i.e., any criminal origin, including
without limitation, corruption, drug trafficking, organized crime, terrorism, money laundering
or fraud); or
隐匿或掩饰财产的非法来源（即任何犯罪来源，包括但不限于腐败、贩毒、有组织犯罪、
恐怖主义、洗钱或欺诈）；或
(b)
assisting any person who is involved in the commission of the criminal offence as a result of
which such property is generated, to evade the legal consequences of such actions.

“FORCE MAJEURE EVENT” means any event or circumstance or combination thereof occurring
after the EFFECTIVE DATE:
“不可抗力事件”指在生效日期之后发生的任何事件或情况，或其组合，该事件或情况：
(a)
the occurrence of which is not reasonably foreseeable as of the EFFECTIVE DATE; and
在生效日期时不可合理预见；且
(b)
which is beyond the reasonable control of the PARTY affected by such event or circumstance
and which such affected PARTY is unable to prevent or overcome (notwithstanding the
reasonable care of the PARTY affected),
超出受影响一方的合理控制范围，且该受影响一方即使已采取合理注意仍无法防止或克
服，
and shall include:
并包括：
(i)
flood, lightning, storm, typhoon, tornado, earthquake, landslide, soil erosion,
subsidence, washout, radioactive contamination (other than that caused by the use of
radioactive sources by the CONTRACTOR, the COMPANY, SUBCONTRACTORS
and/or OTHER CONTRACTORS in connection with the performance of either
PARTY’s obligations under this AGREEMENT), or epidemic;
洪水、雷电、风暴、台风、龙卷风、地震、滑坡、水土流失、地面沉降、冲毁、
放射性污染（但因承包商、公司、分包商和/或其他承包商在履行任何一方本合
同项下义务过程中使用放射性来源所造成的除外）、或流行病；
(ii)
war (whether declared or undeclared), blockade, insurrection, military uprising, or act
of public enemies;
战争（无论是否宣战）、封锁、叛乱、军事起义或敌对行为；
(iii)
revolution, rebellion, civil war, riot, civil disturbance, civil commotion, terrorist acts,
seizure or act of sabotage, imposition by a GOVERNMENTAL AUTHORITY of
sanctions, embargo or breaking off of diplomatic relations by a GOVERNMENTAL
AUTHORITY; and
革命、叛乱、内战、暴动、社会骚乱、社会动乱、恐怖主义行为、扣押或破坏
行为、政府机构实施的制裁、禁运或政府机构断绝外交关系；以及
(iv)
trade or labour dispute, strike, lockout, industrial disturbance, except where limited to
the CONTRACTOR PERSONNEL,
贸易或劳资纠纷、罢工、停工、产业骚乱，但不包括仅限于承包商人员的情形，
other than, in each case, to the extent caused by the PARTY seeking to rely on Article 38.1, or
its AFFILIATES, SUBCONTRACTORS or PERSONNEL, and provided, in each case, that
the event or circumstance in question satisfies the requirements of paragraphs (a) and (b)
above.
但在任何情况下，如该事件或情形系因主张依赖第38.1 条的一方或其关联方、分包商或
人员所导致，则不构成不可抗力事件，并且在任何情况下，该事件或情形必须符合上述
(a)和(b)的要求。

INTERPRETATION AND CONSTRUCTION
解释与释义
In the AGREEMENT (unless the context otherwise requires or where it is expressly stated to the
contrary):
在本合同中（除非上下文另有要求或另有明确规定）：
words denoting the singular number only shall include the plural and vice versa; words
denoting the masculine shall include the feminine and vice versa;
单数词语应包括复数，反之亦然；阳性词语应包括阴性，反之亦然；
references to Articles shall be construed as references to Articles of these GENERAL TERMS
AND CONDITIONS;
对相关条款的引用应解释为对本通用条款与条件之条款的引用；
headings shall be for convenience of reference only and shall not affect the interpretation of
any provision hereof;
标题仅为查阅方便之用，不应影响本合同任何条款的解释；

CONFLICTS AND INCONSISTENCIES BETWEEN AGREEMENT DOCUMENTS
合同文件之间的冲突与不一致
If there is any error, fault, conflict, ambiguity or discrepancy arising within the AGREEMENT
DOCUMENTS that cannot be resolved by applying the order of precedence in Clause 1.6 of
the FORM OF AGREEMENT, the PARTY discovering the same shall notify the other in
writing promptly following its discovery and the COMPANY shall thereafter issue an
instruction to the CONTRACTOR to resolve the inconsistency.
若合同文件中出现任何错误、瑕疵、冲突、含糊或矛盾，而无法通过适用合同协议书第
1.6 条所规定的优先顺序予以解决，则发现该情形的一方应在发现后及时书面通知另一
方，公司随后应向承包商发出指令以解决该不一致。

COMPLIANCE WITH THE ICV IMPROVEMENT PLAN
遵守ICV 提升计划
The CONTRACTOR shall comply with the terms of the ICV IMPROVEMENT PLAN and
confirms that the annual targets set out in the ICV IMPROVEMENT PLAN shall be achieved
by the dates specified therein.
承包商应遵守ICV 提升计划的条款，并确认应在该计划中规定的日期实现ICV 提升计
划所列年度目标。
Upon the CONTRACTOR’s achievement, partially or in full, of the annual target set out in
the ICV IMPROVEMENT PLAN for the particular year (“ANNUAL ICV TARGET”), the
CONTRACTOR shall be entitled to payment of a portion of the ICV PAYMENT for that year
calculated pro-rata based on the percentage of the ANNUAL ICV TARGET achieved,
provided that the CONTRACTOR shall not be entitled to any ICV PAYMENT for any targets
set out in the ICV IMPROVEMENT PLAN that have not been achieved by FINAL
ACCEPTANCE.
在承包商实现ICV 提升计划中所列当年年度目标的全部或部分时（“年度ICV 目标”），
承包商有权按比例获得当年ICV 付款，其金额按已完成的年度ICV 目标比例计算。但
承包商对于直至最终验收时仍未实现的ICV 提升计划中所列目标，不得获得任何ICV
付款。

NUISANCE & TRESPASS
妨害与侵入
The CONTRACTOR shall not commit any trespass or actionable public or private nuisance or
other infringement of the legal rights of THIRD PARTIES potentially affected by the
WORKS.
The CONTRACTOR shall take all protective measures as may be required to
avoid any such trespass, nuisance or infringement, and shall adopt appropriate means for
carrying out the WORKS to that end.
承包商不得实施任何侵入、可诉的公共或私人妨害，或其他可能因合同工程而影响第三
方法律权利的侵害行为。承包商应采取一切必要防护措施以避免该等侵入、妨害或侵害，
并应采用适当方法实施合同工程。
The CONTRACTOR shall take all reasonable steps to prevent or mitigate any interference
caused by the WORKS to the convenience of the public, including the public’s ability to use
public or private roads, footpaths, car parking areas and waterways including locks, docks, sea
walls and other structures relating to waterways.

众使用公共或私人道路、人行道、停车场和水路（包括船闸、码头、防波堤及其他水利
设施）的能力。

SITES.

Unless otherwise provided in the AGREEMENT, none of the WORKS shall be carried out
during the night or on public holidays applicable to the private sector in the UAE without the
prior written consent of the COMPANY, except where such work is both (i) permitted by
applicable LAW and (ii) necessary or required to ensure the safety of the WORKS, or for the
protection of life, or to prevent loss of or damage to property, in which case the
CONTRACTOR shall immediately notify the COMPANY in writing.
除非本合同另有规定，未经公司事先书面同意，承包商不得在阿联酋适用于私营部门的
公共假日或夜间开展合同工程。但若该等工作同时符合以下条件，则例外：(i)相关法律
所允许；且(ii)为确保合同工程安全、保护生命或防止财产灭失或损害所必要或必须。在
此情况下，承包商应立即书面通知公司。

生了重大变更，且该等变更并非因承包商所致；及
where either Article 12.10(a) or 12.10(b) applies, such obligations have had or will have a
verifiable adverse impact on the CONTRACTOR’s costs and/or its ability to achieve any
KEY MILESTONES,
在第12.10(a)条或第12.10(b)条适用的情况下，该等义务已对承包商的成本和/或其实现
任何关键里程碑的能力造成或将造成可验证的不利影响，
and the CONTRACTOR provides evidence that it has undertaken or shall undertake reasonable
mitigation efforts to minimise the adverse consequences of the same, in which case the
CONTRACTOR shall be entitled, subject to Article 24, to submit a CONTRACTOR VARIATION
REQUEST to address any resulting cost of performing and/or delay to the WORKS.
并且承包商提供证据证明其已采取或将采取合理的缓解措施，以尽量减少该等不利后果。在此情
况下，承包商有权在遵守第24 条的前提下，提交承包商变更请求，以处理由此产生的履行成本
和/或合同工程延误。
Without limiting any other obligations of the CONTRACTOR under the AGREEMENT, the
CONTRACTOR shall comply with the interface procedures set out in ANNEXURE 3.
在不限制承包商于本合同项下其他义务的前提下，承包商应遵守附件3 所规定的界面程序。

INSPECTION & TESTING 检验与试验
The COMPANY shall have the right at all times to supervise the performance of the WORKS by the
CONTRACTOR.
The supervision of the WORKS by the COMPANY shall not be deemed
acceptance by the COMPANY of the WORKS or any applicable MILESTONE, and shall not relieve
the CONTRACTOR of any of its obligations under the AGREEMENT or LAW.
公司有权随时监督承包商的合同工程履行情况。公司对合同工程的监督不得视为公司对合同工
程或任何相关里程碑的验收，亦不得免除承包商在本合同或法律项下的任何义务。
The CONTRACTOR shall conduct all tests and inspections in accordance with GOOD INDUSTRY
PRACTICE.
The detailed obligations of the COMPANY and the CONTRACTOR in relation to tests
and inspections are set out in ANNEXURE 10D.
承包商应依照良好行业惯例实施所有试验和检验。公司与承包商在试验与检验方面的详细义务
见附件10D。
The CONTRACTOR shall:
承包商应：
notify the COMPANY of its intention to undertake tests or inspections:
通知公司其拟展开试验或检验的意向：

at the SITE or any other location (including any CONTRACTOR SITE) in the UAE,
not less than forty-eight (48) hours prior to the scheduled date of the relevant test or
inspection; and
在阿联酋境内的现场或任何其他地点（包括任何承包商场地），不得迟于相关
试验或检验计划日期前四十八（48）小时；及
(ii)
at any location outside the UAE, not less than seven (7) DAYS prior to the scheduled
date of the relevant test or inspection;
在阿联酋境外的任何地点，不得迟于相关试验或检验计划日期前七（7）日；
advise the COMPANY sufficiently in advance of the arrangements to be made for said tests or
inspections;
提前充分告知公司为该等试验或检验所作安排；
conduct such testing or inspections in the presence of the COMPANY and, if required by the
COMPANY, its representatives and/or any independent inspectors nominated by either or
both of the PARTIES;
在公司在场的情况下进行该等试验或检验，且如公司要求，应在其代表和/或由双方之一
或双方共同指定的独立检验员在场的情况下进行；
supply all necessary MATERIALS and CONTRACTOR CONSTRUCTION EQUIPMENT
for any tests or inspections required under the AGREEMENT;
提供本合同要求的试验或检验所需的全部必要材料和承包商施工设备；
obtain approval from the COMPANY in advance for all CONTRACTOR CONSTRUCTION
EQUIPMENT and MATERIALS to be used for the purpose of performing tests or inspections;
and

START-UP, PERFORMANCE TESTS & PERFORMANCE GUARANTEES 开车、性能试验与

START-UP
开车
Following issuance of the READY FOR START-UP CERTIFICATE, the COMPANY may
perform START-UP in accordance with ANNEXURE 10D and the documentation approved
pursuant to Article 15.3(a). The CONTRACTOR shall provide such support, manpower and
assistance as the COMPANY may request for the purposes of START-UP.
在具备开车条件证书签发后，公司可依照附件10D 以及依第15.3(a)条批准的文件实施开
车。承包商应根据公司要求，提供开车所需的支持、人力和协助。
During START-UP, the COMPANY shall notify the CONTRACTOR in writing of any
DEFECTS (including any outstanding PUNCH LIST ITEMS) that render the relevant
SYSTEM or the FACILITIES (as applicable) unsuitable for the introduction of FEEDSTOCK
or for carrying out the PERFORMANCE TESTS or that cause or will cause the FACILITIES
to fail to achieve PROVISIONAL ACCEPTANCE, following which the CONTRACTOR
shall correct such DEFECTS at its own risk and cost.
在开车过程中，如公司发现或认为存在任何缺陷（包括任何尚未完成的整改清单事项），
致使相关系统或工程设施（视情况而定）不适合引入原料、进行性能试验，或导致/将导
致工程设施未能实现初步验收，公司应书面通知承包商。承包商应自担风险和费用修复
该等缺陷。

PERFORMANCE GUARANTEES
性能保证
The CONTRACTOR guarantees that during the PERFORMANCE TESTS, the FACILITIES
or
parts
thereof
to
which
the

TESTS

承包商保证，在性能试验期间，工程设施或其相关部分应达到附件11 所列的性能保证。
If a PERFORMANCE GUARANTEE is not attained during any PERFORMANCE TEST (or
repetition thereof), the CONTRACTOR shall make all appropriate adjustments, modifications
or additions to the SYSTEM(s) or the FACILITIES as may be necessary in order for the
PERFORMANCE GUARANTEE to be attained, and the CONTRACTOR shall thereafter
repeat the PERFORMANCE TESTS until all of the PERFORMANCE GUARANTEES are
attained.

(and to the extent) that the need for such adjustments, modifications or additions was
attributable to the COMPANY.
如在任何性能试验（或重复试验）中未达到性能保证，承包商应对系统或工程设施进行
所有必要的调整、改造或补充，以实现性能保证，并应随后重复性能试验，直至全部性
能保证均被实现。承包商应对相关费用与支出负责，除非（且在限度范围内）该等调整、
改造或补充系因公司所致。
Where the CONTRACTOR has performed the PERFORMANCE TESTS on three (3) or more
occasions (including two (2) PERFORMANCE TEST repetitions) and has not attained the
PERFORMANCE GUARANTEES in the most recent PERFORMANCE TEST, then the
COMPANY may elect, at its sole discretion, to require the CONTRACTOR either to:
若承包商已进行三（3）次或以上性能试验（含两（2）次重复试验），且在最近一次性
能试验中仍未实现性能保证，则公司可自行决定要求承包商：
(i)
continue to make such changes, modifications and/or additions to the FACILITIES or
any part thereof pursuant to Article 16.3(b) or which otherwise may be necessary in
order to attain the PERFORMANCE GUARANTEES; or
按第16.3(b)条的规定，或以其他必要方式，继续对工程设施或其任何部分进行
改造、调整和/或补充，以实现性能保证；或
(ii)
subject to Article 17.1, issue the PROVISIONAL ACCEPTANCE CERTIFICATE.
在遵守第17.1 条的前提下，签发初步验收证书。

17.
PROVISIONAL ACCEPTANCE & FINAL ACCEPTANCE 初步验收与最终验收
PROVISIONAL ACCEPTANCE
初步验收
PROVISIONAL ACCEPTANCE shall occur whenever the following conditions have been
met, in relation to a part or the whole of the WORKS, as applicable:
若满足以下条件，则就合同工程的部分或整体（视情况而定）视为已实现初步验收：
(i)
MECHANICAL COMPLETION and COMMISSIONING of all SYSTEMS and the
FACILITIES has been completed successfully;
所有系统和工程设施的机械竣工与试车已顺利完成；
(ii)
START-UP for the SYSTEMS has been successfully completed;
系统的开车已顺利完成；
(iii)
the PERFORMANCE TESTS have been completed and, subject to Article 16.3, the
FACILITIES have attained all of the PERFORMANCE GUARANTEES;
性能试验已完成，且依照第16.3 条，工程设施已达到全部性能保证；
(iv)
all PUNCH LIST ITEMS required by the SCOPE OF WORK to have been rectified
as a condition to PROVISIONAL ACCEPTANCE have been rectified and the
FACILITIES have otherwise been completed in accordance with the AGREEMENT
(save for any PUNCH LIST ITEMS which are not required by the SCOPE OF
WORK to have been rectified as a condition to PROVISIONAL ACCEPTANCE);
工作范围要求作为初步验收条件而需整改的所有整改清单事项均已整改完毕，
且工程设施已依照本合同完成（不包括工作范围未要求作为初步验收条件的整
改清单事项）；

TIMELY PERFORMANCE, WORK PROGRAM, PROGRESS REPORTING & DELAYS 按
时履行、进度计划、进度报告与延误
TIMELY PERFORMANCE
按时履行
The CONTRACTOR shall commence the WORKS on the COMMENCEMENT DATE.
承包商应于开工日期开始实施合同工程。
The CONTRACTOR shall perform and complete the WORKS with due expedition.
承包商应以应有的效率实施并完成合同工程。
The CONTRACTOR shall comply with the WORK PROGRAM, including by achieving each
KEY MILESTONE by its associated KEY MILESTONE DATE.
In the event of any
conflict or discrepancy between a date in the WORK PROGRAM and a KEY MILESTONE
DATE, the KEY MILESTONE DATE shall prevail.
承包商应遵守进度计划，包括在各关键里程碑日期完成相应的关键里程碑。如进度计划
中的日期与关键里程碑日期存在冲突或不一致，以关键里程碑日期为准。
WORK PROGRAM
进度计划
Within thirty (30) DAYS of the COMMENCEMENT DATE, the CONTRACTOR shall
prepare and submit to the COMPANY a WORK PROGRAM that is consistent with the
schedule set out in ANNEXURE 4 and shows in detail the timing and sequencing (including
with logic links) of individual activities by which the CONTRACTOR proposes to carry out
the WORKS.
If the WORK PROGRAM does not meet the said requirements, the
COMPANY may require the CONTRACTOR to correct it at the CONTRACTOR’s cost, and
without the CONTRACTOR becoming entitled to any adjustments to the AGREEMENT
PRICE and/or the KEY MILESTONE DATES, or other relief.
For the avoidance of doubt,
no correction required pursuant to this Article 18.2(a) shall be considered as a VARIATION.
承包商应于开工日期起三十（30）日内编制并提交进度计划，确保其与附件4 所列进度
一致，并详细显示承包商拟实施合同工程的时间和顺序（包括逻辑关系）。如进度计划
未满足上述要求，公司可要求承包商自费予以修正，且承包商无权因此获得合同价格和
/或关键里程碑日期的调整或其他救济。为免生疑义，承包商依本第18.2(a)条所作修正不
得视为变更。
The CONTRACTOR shall continuously monitor the progress of all activities specified in the
WORK PROGRAM and shall submit a detailed PROGRESS REPORT to the COMPANY
every month pursuant to Article 18.3, accompanied by an updated WORK PROGRAM
consistent with the requirements of Article 18.2(a) and depicting a realistic projection for the
future progress of the WORKS (extending to the achievement of PROVISIONAL

进度报告
In addition to delivering the reports required under ANNEXURE 10B (at the times and in the manner

COMPANY (along with an updated WORK PROGRAM pursuant to Article 18.2) a written report
showing the progress of the WORKS during the immediately preceding monthly period, in a form
satisfactory to the COMPANY (each a “PROGRESS REPORT”). A PROGRESS REPORT shall
address or contain (as the case may be) the following:
除了承包商应按附件10B（在其中规定的时间和方式）提交的报告外，承包商还应于每月第5
个工作日，向公司提交书面报告（并附依第18.2 条规定更新的进度计划），显示上一月度期间
合同工程的进展情况，且报告形式须令公司满意（“进度报告”）。进度报告应包括或载明（视情
况而定）：
for each activity, the percentage completion achieved compared with the planned percentage
completion as set out in the previous month’s update of the WORK PROGRAM;
各项活动的完成百分比，与上月更新进度计划所列的计划完成百分比对比；
if the progress of any activity is delayed relative to the dates indicated on the previous month’s
update of the WORK PROGRAM, an explanation of the reasons for, and likely consequences
of, such delay, and stating the corrective action being taken to remedy the delay;
如任何活动的进展较上月更新进度计划所示日期延误，应说明延误原因、可能后果，并
列明正在采取的纠正措施；
information on all critical aspects influencing the progress of the WORKS;
影响合同工程进展的所有关键因素的信息；
a general report on the status of the WORKS; and
合同工程的总体状态报告；及
such other information as the COMPANY reasonably requires from time to time.

In the event that the CONTRACTOR fails to attain a KEY MILESTONE by its associated
KEY MILESTONE DATE, the CONTRACTOR shall (within thirty (30) DAYS of being
notified by the COMPANY) pay DELAY LIQUIDATED DAMAGES to the COMPANY in
the amount set out in the FORM OF AGREEMENT for each week (or part thereof) of delay,
up to the relevant agreed overall maximum amount as set out in the FORM OF
AGREEMENT. DELAY LIQUIDATED DAMAGES shall accrue in respect of each week of
delay or part thereof and shall be a debt in lieu of the CONTRACTOR’s performance of its
obligation to timely achieve the corresponding KEY MILESTONES.
如承包商未能在关键里程碑日期实现某一关键里程碑，承包商应于公司通知后三十（30）
日内，向公司支付合同协议书规定的延误违约金，其金额按每延误一周（或不足一周的
部分）计算，直至达到合同协议书规定的相关延误违约金总额上限。延误违约金按每周
或不足一周的延误累计计取，并构成承包商未能按时实现关键里程碑时应履行义务的替
代性债务。

承包商人员（一般规定）
The CONTRACTOR shall provide and employ on the SITE, and in any other workplace in
connection with the performance of the WORKS, such professional, experienced, skilled,
semiskilled and unskilled labour and local employees as are necessary for the proper and
timely performance and completion of the WORKS. The CONTRACTOR shall ensure that

personnel who meet the same standards provided for in this Article 19.1(a).

demobilisation of CONTRACTOR PERSONNEL, including by arranging all CONSENTS

any breach of the AGREEMENT or act of prevention by the COMPANY, its AFFILIATES or
their respective agents or employees;
公司、其关联方或各自代理人或雇员违反本合同或实施阻碍行为；
subject to Article 12.10, any delays caused by ASSOCIATED WORKS CONTRACTORS
working at the SITE, except to the extent that the delays caused by ASSOCIATED WORKS
CONTRACTORS are attributable to the CONTRACTOR’s breach of Article 12;
在遵守第12.10 条的前提下，因相关工程承包商在现场施工造成的任何延误，但该等延

the circumstances referred to in Articles 4.3(c), 5.2(a), 7.9(e), 8.4, 11.10(b), 11.11(b) 13.4,
23.7(c) or 29.1;
第4.3(c)、5.2(a)、7.9(e)、8.4、11.10(b)、11.11(b)、13.4、23.7(c)或29.1 条所述情况；
a suspension of the WORKS in the circumstances referred to in Article 34.1 or 34.3;

the implementation of works constituting a VARIATION pursuant to Article 24;

29.
CHANGE IN LAW 法律变更
Subject to Article 29.2, if a change in LAW in the UAE comes into effect after the FINAL
COMMERCIAL BID SUBMISSION DATE and increases the CONTRACTOR’s costs in performing
its obligations under the AGREEMENT and/or delays the CONTRACTOR’s performance of the
WORKS, the CONTRACTOR shall, subject to Article 24, be entitled to submit a CONTRACTOR
VARIATION REQUEST to recover any costs or delays incurred as a result of such change in LAW,
provided that the CONTRACTOR has used reasonable and lawful endeavours to mitigate the effects of
such change in LAW.
在遵守第29.2 条的前提下，如在最终商务标提交日期之后阿联酋生效的法律变更导致承包商履
行本合同义务的成本增加和/或履行合同工程延误，承包商在遵守第24 条的前提下，有权提交承
包商变更请求，以追回因该法律变更而发生的任何成本或延误，前提是承包商已采取合理且合
法的措施减轻该法律变更的影响。
The following shall not be considered changes in LAW and the CONTRACTOR shall not be entitled to
any adjustments to the AGREEMENT PRICE and/or the KEY MILESTONE DATES, or other relief
pursuant to Article 29.1 in relation to any of them:

changes in LAW applicable outside the UAE; and

PROCEDURE FOR DEFENCE OF INDEMNIFIED CLAIMS
受偿索赔的抗辩程序
If either PARTY becomes aware of any claim in respect of any of the events described in
Articles 4.3(e), 20.6, 22.3, 41.12, 41.16(d) and 42.1 to 42.5 (“INDEMNIFIED CLAIM”),
such PARTY shall promptly provide notice thereof in writing to the other PARTY.
Such
notice shall include as much detail as is reasonably practicable regarding the INDEMNIFIED
CLAIM. Notwithstanding the foregoing, lack of prompt notice shall not be a defence to
indemnification.
如任一方知悉发生第4.3(e)、第20.6、第22.3、第41.12、第41.16(d)及第42.1 至第42.5
条所述事件的任何相关索赔（“受偿索赔”），该一方应立即书面通知另一方。该通知应
在合理可行范围内尽可能详述受偿索赔的细节。尽管有前述规定，未及时通知不构成拒
绝赔偿的抗辩理由。
Upon receiving written notice of any INDEMNIFIED CLAIM, the INDEMNIFYING PARTY
may request permission from the INDEMNIFIED PARTY to assume control of the defence of
the INDEMNIFIED CLAIM. The INDEMNIFIED PARTY shall promptly decide, at its sole
discretion, whether to grant the permission sought by the INDEMNIFYING PARTY,
provided that the INDEMNIFIED PARTY’s silence, or any delay in response, shall not be
deemed as an acceptance to grant the permission sought by the INDEMNIFYING PARTY.
在收到任何受偿索赔的书面通知后，赔偿方可请求受偿方同意其接管该受偿索赔的抗辩。
受偿方应尽快自行决定是否同意，但受偿方的沉默或迟延回复不应视为同意。
Subject to Article 42.7(e), in the event the INDEMNIFIED PARTY grants permission under
Article 42.7(b), the INDEMNIFYING PARTY shall have control of the conduct of the
defence of the INDEMNIFIED PARTY, including but not limited to the selection and
management of counsel. An INDEMNIFYING PARTY who assumes control of the defence
shall be responsible for paying the INDEMNIFIED PARTY’s reasonable defence costs,
including attorneys’ fees, on a current basis and throughout the pendency of any such
INDEMNIFIED CLAIMS. In this case, the INDEMNIFIED PARTY shall have the right, at
its sole discretion, to be represented by advisory counsel of its own selection and at its own
cost and to monitor the progress and handling of an INDEMNIFIED CLAIM.

and management of counsel. The CONTRACTOR hereby agrees, and shall ensure that the
CONTRACTOR INDEMNIFIED PARTIES agree, to the COMPANY assuming control of
the defence of such claim.
Upon assuming control of the defence in relation to any such
claim, the COMPANY shall be subrogated to the rights and liabilities of the CONTRACTOR
INDEMNIFIED PARTIES in relation to the claim, including with respect to the damages
payable or owing as a result of any such claim.
For the avoidance of doubt, such subrogation
shall include the COMPANY’s assumption of responsibility for paying the CONTRACTOR
INDEMNIFIED PARTIES’ defence costs, including attorney’s fees, on a current basis and
throughout the pendency of the claim. For the avoidance of doubt, the COMPANY shall only
be subrogated to the rights and obligations of the CONTRACTOR INDEMNIFIED PARTIES
in relation to the claim where the COMPANY assumes control of the defence of claims in
accordance with this Article 42.7(e).
尽管第42.7 条另有规定，如任何主体就下列情形向承包商受偿方提出主张：(i)发生于阿
布扎比；(ii)因本合同引起或与之相关；或(iii)涉及公司受偿方的财产，公司可自行决定
接管该主张之抗辩，包括但不限于律师之选择与管理。承包商在此同意，并应确保承包
商受偿方同意由公司接管该等抗辩。一旦公司接管与该等主张相关之抗辩，公司即就该

ASSIGNMENT 转让
The CONTRACTOR shall not assign, novate or otherwise transfer any of its rights, obligations or
interests under and in connection with the AGREEMENT without the prior written consent of the
COMPANY. Any purported assignment, novation, or transfer without the prior written consent of the
COMPANY shall be expressly deemed absolutely null and void against the COMPANY and shall not
transfer any rights in the AGREEMENT to the purported assignee and, in such event, the COMPANY
shall have no obligation whatsoever to the purported assignee. Any purported assignment, novation, or
transfer without the prior written consent of the COMPANY shall entitle the COMPANY to terminate
the AGREEMENT with immediate effect in accordance with Article 35.3(a)(i).
未经公司事先书面同意，承包商不得转让、更新或以其他方式转移其在本合同项下及与本合同相
关的任何权利、义务或权益。任何未经公司事先书面同意的拟议转让、更新或转移应被明确视为
对公司绝对无效，不得使拟议受让人获得本合同项下的任何权利；在此情形下，公司对该拟议受
让人不承担任何义务。任何未经公司事先书面同意的拟议转让、更新或转移均赋予公司依第
35.3(a)(i)条立即终止本合同的权利。

under and in connection with the AGREEMENT to any AFFILIATE upon written notice to the
CONTRACTOR, and the CONTRACTOR hereby consents unconditionally to such assignment,
novation or transfer. The CONTRACTOR shall execute any documentation required to ensure that the
assignment is properly executed in accordance with the applicable LAW.

本合同受阿布扎比法律及阿联酋联邦法律管辖，并依其进行解释。
DISPUTE RESOLUTION
争议解决
The PARTIES shall attempt in good faith to amicably settle any dispute, controversy or claim
arising out of or in connection with the conclusion, validity, effect, interpretation, performance,
termination or dissolution of the AGREEMENT and/or any non-contractual obligations
arising out of or in connection with the AGREEMENT (“DISPUTE”). The PARTIES agree
that all discussions during attempts to settle DISPUTES amicably will be on a “without
prejudice” basis and shall not be admissible as evidence in any arbitral proceedings initiated
under Article 46.2(b).
双方应本着诚信原则，尽力友好协商解决因本合同的订立、效力、解释、履行、终止或
解除，及/或因本合同引起或与本合同相关的非合同义务而产生的任何争议、纠纷或索赔
（“争议”）。双方同意，在尝试友好解决争议期间的所有讨论均属“无损害”性质，且不
得作为依第46.2(b)条启动的任何仲裁程序中的证据。
If the DISPUTE is not settled amicably within sixty (60) DAYS from the date the DISPUTE
is first notified in writing to the other PARTY, such DISPUTE may be referred to by any
PARTY and finally resolved by arbitration in accordance with the rules of arbitration of the
International
Chamber
of
Commerce
applicable
at
the
time
of
conclusion
of
the
AGREEMENT (the “RULES”) by three (3) arbitrators. Each PARTY shall nominate an
arbitrator for confirmation by the International Court of Arbitration (the “COURT”) under the
RULES. The two arbitrators nominated by the PARTIES shall nominate the third arbitrator
who will act as president of the arbitral tribunal (the “PRESIDENT”) within thirty (30)
DAYS from their confirmation by the COURT. Failing nomination by the arbitrators of the
PRESIDENT within the time limit provided for in this Article or any other time limit agreed
by the PARTIES, the PRESIDENT shall be appointed by the COURT.
如该争议自一方向另一方首次书面通知之日起六十（60）日内未能友好解决，则任何一
方可将该争议提交并最终依国际商会在本合同订立时适用的仲裁规则（“规则”）通过三
（3）名仲裁员仲裁解决。每一方应指定一名仲裁员，经国际仲裁院（“仲裁院”）确认。
双方指定的两名仲裁员应在仲裁院确认之日起三十（30）日内共同指定第三名仲裁员担
任仲裁庭主席（“主席”）。如仲裁员未能在本条所规定或双方另行约定的期限内指定主
席，则由仲裁院指定主席。
If the DISPUTE is not settled amicably within sixty (60) DAYS from the date the DISPUTE
is first notified in writing to the other PARTY or PARTIES pursuant to Article 46.2, and
provided the DISPUTE has not been referred to arbitration pursuant to Article 46.2(b), the
PARTIES may mutually agree in writing at any time to refer a DISPUTE to be resolved by an
alternative forum for resolution including mediation or expert determination. The PARTIES
agree that all discussions and proceedings thereunder will be on a “without prejudice” basis
and shall not be admissible as evidence in any arbitral proceedings initiated under
Article 46.2(b).
如该争议自依第46.2 条首次书面通知另一方之日起六十（60）日内未能友好解决，且未
依第46.2(b)条将争议提交仲裁，双方可在任何时候书面共同同意，将争议提交其他替代
性解决机制，包括调解或专家裁决。双方同意，相关讨论与程序均属“无损害”性质，且
不得作为依第46.2(b)条启动的任何仲裁程序中的证据。
A PARTY may apply to any competent judicial authority for interim or conservatory relief.
The application for such measures or the enforcement of such measures ordered by such
judicial authority shall not be deemed an infringement or waiver of the agreement to arbitrate
and shall not affect the powers of the arbitrators.

ARBITRATION
INFORMATION, which it deems necessary for the purposes of those proceedings, (iv) after
seeking an appropriate confidentiality order from such court, if available under the applicable
LAW.
仲裁程序，包括所有文件、陈述、书面和口头证据、记录和往来函件，以及相关的任何
裁定或裁决，均应严格保密（“仲裁保密信息”）。但在不影响任何其他适用的保密义务
的前提下，任何一方可在国内法院进行撤销和/或执行程序时，向该国内法院披露其认为
为上述程序所必需的仲裁保密信息，前提是（如适用法律允许）已向该法院申请适当的
保密令。
For the avoidance of doubt, where a DISPUTE is not settled amicably under Article 46.2(a)
and a PARTY elects to refer such DISPUTE to arbitration under Article 46.2(b), such PARTY
acknowledges that arbitration shall be the final platform for resolution of the DISPUTE.
为免生疑义，如争议未依第46.2(a)条友好解决，且一方选择依第46.2(b)条将争议提交仲
裁，该一方确认仲裁应为解决该争议的最终平台。
47.
CONFLICT OF INTEREST 利益冲突
The CONTRACTOR shall not, and shall ensure that its AFFILIATES do not, enter into any contracts,
undertakings, bids, letters of intent, business associations, joint ventures, partnership or other
arrangements (each a “BUSINESS VENTURE”) directly relating to any aspect of the WORKS,
except for those disclosed to the COMPANY prior to the EFFECTIVE DATE, without obtaining the
COMPANY’s prior written consent, which consent shall not unreasonably be withheld.
承包商不得，且应确保其关联方不得，在未经公司事先书面同意的情况下（该同意不应被无理拒
绝），订立与合同工程任何方面直接相关的任何合同、承诺、投标、意向书、业务合作、合资、
合伙或其他安排（“商业合作”），除非该等事项已在生效日期前向公司披露。
If the COMPANY deems (at its absolute discretion) that any BUSINESS VENTURE by the
CONTRACTOR under this Article 47 is prejudicial to the COMPANY’s interest, the COMPANY shall
instruct the CONTRACTOR in writing to take steps to terminate such BUSINESS VENTURE, and the
CONTRACTOR shall comply with the COMPANY’s instruction.
如公司（自行全权决定）认定承包商依本第47 条进行的任何商业合作有损于公司利益，公司应
书面指示承包商采取措施终止该商业合作，承包商应遵守公司的该项指示。
48.
ENTIRE AGREEMENT 完整协议
The AGREEMENT constitutes the entire agreement between the PARTIES concerning the subject
matter of the AGREEMENT and supersedes and, subject to Article 3.2, replaces any previous
agreements between the PARTIES or any representation made by one PARTY to the other PARTY

Each PARTY represents and warrants to the other PARTY that neither it nor its respective

曾且承诺将不会作出或承诺作出任何支付金钱或给予任何有价物品之行为：
any GOVERNMENT OFFICIAL, for the purpose of securing or inducing the act, decision,
influence, or omission of such GOVERNMENT OFFICIAL to obtain, retain, or direct
business, or secure any improper advantage, for any PERSON in connection with the
AGREEMENT or any other asset or business opportunity of the PARTIES; or
向任何政府官员支付，以获取或诱使该政府官员作出或不作出行为、作出决定或施加影

业务，或获得任何不正当利益；或
any PERSON while knowing, believing or being aware of a high probability that the payment
or other value would be offered, given or promised by such PERSON in whole or in part to

influence or omission of such GOVERNMENT OFFICIAL to obtain, retain or direct business,
or secure any improper advantage, for any PERSON in connection with the AGREEMENT or
any other asset or business opportunity of the PARTIES.

extent possible, achieves the intended commercial result of the original provision.

可能为非法、无效或不可执行，双方应在十四（14）日内本着诚信原则进行协商，以修改该条款，
使其在修改后合法、有效并可执行，且在最大程度上实现原条款所预期的商业意图。

shall be an original, and all the counterparts together shall constitute one and the same instrument.

Preference to Local Manufacturers:
优先使用本地制造商：
CONTRACTOR shall promote and give the first preference to the UAE based manufacturers for all
products and equipment required for PROJECT based on a total cost of delivery to the Project site.
Accordingly, preference shall be given by CONTRACTOR to UAE based manufacturing Entities (or
their Agents) over Manufacturers located outside of the UAE (or their Agents).
承包商应优先考虑并选择阿联酋本地制造商作为项目所需全部产品和设备的供应方，依据的标准
为交付至项目现场的总成本。据此，承包商应优先选择阿联酋境内的制造企业（或其代理）而非
阿联酋境外的制造商（或其代理）。
In case manufacturers or vendors located outside of the UAE are finally shortlisted for award,
CONTRACTOR will be required to provide an explanation and evidence to substantiate these findings
for COMPANY approval, prior to proceeding with the purchase.
如最终入围授标的制造商或供应商为阿联酋境外企业，承包商须在采购前向公司提交说明及证据
以证明相关结论，并取得公司批准。
Mandate Fabrication activities to Local Sub Contractors:
将预制活动限定于本地分包商：
CONTRACTOR shall perform all Fabrication activities only within the UAE. In case, they intend to
engage SUBCONTRACTORs for the Fabrication activities, they shall engage only UAE based
companies listed in ADNOC Approved Vendor List (AVL) / ADNOC Approved Contractor List
(ACL).

承包商组织内聘用阿联酋国民
关于承包商在其组织内聘用阿联酋国民的义务，包括但不限于以下内容：
a)
承包商应在其工资成本中，依下文c 项之规定，招聘并留用四十二（42）名“新阿联酋国民”，直至
合同竣工或合同初步验收证书签发，以较早者为准。
b)
该等“新阿联酋国民”应为新增招聘人员，且为承包商组织在“授标函/合同签署日期”已雇佣阿联酋国
民人数之外的新增人员，以较早者为准。
c)
承包商应确保“新阿联酋国民”留在其组织内，并按以下时间表维持最低人数：
自合同生效日期起三个月内：
25 名阿联酋国民
自合同生效日期起六个月内：
42 名阿联酋国民
d)
承包商应自合同生效日期起，每月月底向公司提交一份由阿联酋人力资源与本地化部（MoHRE）下
载的报告，明确说明“新阿联酋国民”的姓名及加入承包商组织的日期。
e)
如承包商未能达到上述人数要求，ADNOC 有权依第XXX 条ICV 付款条款所述机制，从承包商发
票中扣减相应款项。
f)
承包商应制定具体的培训/职业发展计划，除提供全面的在岗培训外，还应为每一名新阿联酋国民分
配导师并予以指导和监督。
g)
在项目完成时，如承包商未能将“新阿联酋国民”继续作为其劳动力的一部分留用，则承包商应协助
该等人员在阿联酋境内找到相同或类似领域的工作。
注：上述(e)项中的“第XXX 条”应为“第3.2 条”。

Preference to Local Manufacturers:
优先使用本地制造商：
CONTRACTOR shall promote and give the first preference to the UAE based manufacturers for all
products and equipment required for PROJECT based on a total cost of delivery to the Project site.
Accordingly, preference shall be given by CONTRACTOR to UAE based manufacturing Entities (or
their Agents) over Manufacturers located outside of the UAE (or their Agents).
承包商应优先考虑并选择阿联酋本地制造商作为项目所需全部产品和设备的供应方，依据的标准
为交付至项目现场的总成本。据此，承包商应优先选择阿联酋境内的制造企业（或其代理）而非
阿联酋境外的制造商（或其代理）。
In case manufacturers or vendors located outside of the UAE are finally shortlisted for award,
CONTRACTOR will be required to provide an explanation and evidence to substantiate these findings
for COMPANY approval, prior to proceeding with the purchase.
如最终入围授标的制造商或供应商为阿联酋境外企业，承包商须在采购前向公司提交说明及证据
以证明相关结论，并取得公司批准。
Mandate Fabrication activities to Local Sub Contractors:
将预制活动限定于本地分包商：
CONTRACTOR shall perform all Fabrication activities only within the UAE. In case, they intend to
engage SUBCONTRACTORs for the Fabrication activities, they shall engage only UAE based
companies listed in ADNOC Approved Vendor List (AVL) / ADNOC Approved Contractor List
(ACL).

承包商应仅在阿联酋境内开展所有预制活动。如拟聘请分包商实施预制活动，则应仅聘用
ADNOC 批准供应商名单（AVL）/ADNOC 批准承包商名单（ACL）中列示的阿联酋本地企业。
In case CONTRACTOR is unable to perform the Fabrication activities in the UAE directly or
indirectly, CONTRACTOR will be required to provide an explanation and evidence to substantiate

A.
申请流程：
1.
在每一份提交的发票中，承包商应申请可支付金额的95%，剩余的5%应作为ICV 付
款预留。
注：合同中的暂定金额不适用于ICV 付款扣减。
2.
根据通用条款与条件的相关条款，承包商应每年提交一次ICV 付款发票，金额应按上
文表A 所列年度ICV 目标完成百分比，按比例计算。
3.
如在最终验收时未能实现任何年度ICV 目标，或仅部分实现，公司将分别：(i)不支付
ICV 付款里程碑金额，或(ii)按照前述第1 点所述流程，基于实际完成百分比按比例支
付相应金额，剩余的ICV 付款里程碑金额由公司保留。
B.
计算公式：
实际ICV%（依项目级ICV 证书确认）
×
当年已付发票总额的5%
计划ICV%（年度ICV 提升计划）
注：每一份ICV 发票均须附有承包商最新的基于项目的ICV 证书作为支持。
C.
ICV 付款计算模板附后。
Payment Deduction Towards Not Meeting the UAE National Numbers

## Page 199

ADNOC-CICV-505A(v6)
198
insert the reference number
ADNOC Classification: Need-To-Know
未达到阿联酋国民人数要求的付款扣减
In the event the Contractor fails to meet the required number of new UAE Nationals stipulated in Clause 2 of
Scope of Work at APPENDIX 6 of RFT / Clause 2.6 of the Scope of Work at ANNEXURE 3 of AGREEMENT,
ADNOC shall deduct the corresponding amounts from Contractor’s payments. The deductions shall be
calculated as under:
如承包商未能达到在RFT附件6工作范围第2条/合同附件3工作范围第2.6条所规定的新聘阿联酋国民人数
要求，ADNOC有权从对承包商的付款中扣除相应金额。扣减的计算方式如下：
(a)
For each headcount below the stipulated targets in Clause 2 of Scope of Work at APPENDIX 6 of
RFT/ Clause 2.1 of the Scope of Work at ANNEXURE 3 of AGREEMENT, the deduction shall be at
the rate of AED 20,000/- per National per month. Such deductions shall commence after 3 months
from the Contract Effective Date.
对于低于RFT 附件6 工作范围第2 条/合同附件3 工作范围第2.1 条所规定目标人数的每一名国
民，按每人每月20,000 迪拉姆的标准扣减。此类扣减自合同生效日期起三个月后开始执行。
(b)
ADNOC shall review the Emirati headcounts on monthly basis (the Emirati headcount required as per
the Scope of Work versus actual headcount as per MOHRE Report in the month). In case of a negative
variance i.e., the actual Emirati headcount as per MOHRE Report are less than the required headcount,
the amount shall be calculated and deducted from Contractor’s invoices.
ADNOC 应按月审查阿联酋国民人数（即工作范围要求的国民人数与当月MoHRE 报告中的实际
人数对比）。如出现负差，即MoHRE 报告所列实际人数少于要求人数，应计算并从承包商发票
中扣除相应金额。
